accordingly:\n\nFor each column `c` in H–L and range `c35:c40`:\n\n- Row 42: `=MIN(c35:c40)`\n- Row 43: `=MAX(c35:c40)`\n- Row 44: `=MEDIAN(c35:c40)`\n- Row 45: `=AVERAGE(c35:c40)`\n- Row 46: H–L:\n\n```excel\n=SUMPRODUCT(c35:c40,c26:c31)/SUM(c26:c31)\n```\n\nExample:\n- `Task!H50`:\n\n```excel\n=SUMPRODUCT(H35:H40,H26:H31)/SUM(H26:H31)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5) Cached values injected (so
